# SGW_Jaffa_Goauld_Ability_Audit_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Jaffa_Goauld_Ability_Audit_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

Sheets:

- [00_README](#00readme)
- [01_Overview](#01overview)
- [02_Jaffa_All_Candidates](#02jaffaallcandidates)
- [03_Jaffa_Branch_Candidates](#03jaffabranchcandidates)
- [04_Jaffa_Tree_Skeleton](#04jaffatreeskeleton)
- [05_Jaffa_Faction_Runtime](#05jaffafactionruntime)
- [06_Goauld_All_Candidates](#06goauldallcandidates)
- [07_Goauld_Branch_Candidates](#07goauldbranchcandidates)
- [08_Goauld_Tree_Skeleton](#08goauldtreeskeleton)
- [09_Goauld_Pet_Runtime](#09goauldpetruntime)
- [10_Moniker_Hypotheses](#10monikerhypotheses)
- [11_Progression_Readiness](#11progressionreadiness)
- [12_Sources](#12sources)

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Purpose | Combined audit for Free Jaffa/Shol'va, Loyal Jaffa and Goa'uld abilities after the human/Asgard class audits. |
| Jaffa branch names | Heritage, Tau'ri and Tactics are explicitly present in recovered trainer-topic text for BOTH SGU Jaffa/Shol'va and Praxis Jaffa. |
| Goa'uld branch names | Ashrak, Battle Lord and Servant Lord are explicitly present in recovered trainer-topic text; historical public descriptions strongly corroborate their roles. |
| Primary ability data | Ability/effect mechanics come from CookedDataAbilities/CookedDataEffects. Trainer topics are secondary evidence for branch names, not final per-node trees. |
| Jaffa caution | No recovered Jaffa branch moniker labels or full Jaffa ability tree were found. Membership is reconstructed from ability names, mechanics, weapons and recurring moniker clusters. |
| Goa'uld caution | Three unknown Cooked moniker clusters align strongly with Ashrak/Battle Lord/Servant Lord, but their textual moniker labels are not directly recovered. |
| Starting abilities | No char_creation_abilities rows were found for the recovered Jaffa/Shol'va/Goa'uld character-definition IDs. Starting skill package is therefore unresolved. |
| Progression | Per-ability unlock level, purchase cost, prerequisites and capstones are NOT yet final. This audit is the prerequisite for building that progression next. |
| Faction runtime | Free Jaffa/Shol'va and Loyal Jaffa share the same three trainer branch names, but physiology/faction handling (Tretonin vs symbiote) must remain separate from the skill-tree branch model. |

_9 data rows, 2 columns._
